<script>
              Array.from(document.querySelectorAll('.navbar-account-dropdown .dropdown-menu a')).forEach(function(link) {
                link.addEventListener('click', function() {
                  document.querySelector('.navbar-account-dropdown').classList.add('force-open');
                })
              });
              //user menu
              document.querySelector('.user-nav-icon').addEventListener('click', function() {
                document.querySelector('html').classList.add('noscroll');
                document.querySelector('.user-navbar-button-inside').classList.remove('hidden');
                document.getElementById('navbar').style.animation = 'userMenuAnimationIn .5s';
                document.querySelector('.sugester-chat-tab').classList.add('hidden');
              });
              document.querySelector('.user-navbar-button-inside').addEventListener('click', function() {
                document.querySelector('html').classList.remove('noscroll');
                document.getElementById('navbar').style.animation = 'userMenuAnimationOut .5s ease 0s 1 normal both running';
                document.getElementById('navbar').classList.add('user-menu-stay-open');
                document.querySelector('.sugester-chat-tab').classList.remove('hidden');
              });
              //inner list choose
              Array.from(document.querySelectorAll('.navbar-account-dropdown .indicator')).forEach(function(indicator) {
                var target = indicator.dataset.target;
                indicator.addEventListener('click', function() {
                  Array.from(document.querySelectorAll('.navbar-account-dropdown .indicator')).forEach(function(indicator) {
                    indicator.dataset.target === target ? indicator.classList.add('active') : indicator.classList.remove('active')
                  });
                  Array.from(document.querySelectorAll('.navbar-account-dropdown .userList')).forEach(function(userList) {
                    userList.classList.contains(target) ? userList.classList.add('active') : userList.classList.remove('active');
                  });
                  computeNotificationsMaxHeight();
                })
              });

              //setting max-height of my account and notifications container
              function computeNotificationsMaxHeight() {
                var heightToReduce = 0;
                var safeMarginFromBottomEdge = 40;
                var mobileToolbarHeight = 50;
                var elementToHeightSet = [document.querySelector('#navbar .notifications'), document.querySelector('#navbar .myAccount')];
                var elementsInsideContainer = [document.querySelector('nav'), document.querySelector('#navbar .special-list'), document.querySelector('#navbar .userMenuChoice'), document.querySelector('#navbar .menu-footer')];
                elementsInsideContainer.forEach(function(element) {
                  if (element) {
                    heightToReduce += element.clientHeight;
                    var finalHeightToReduce = heightToReduce + safeMarginFromBottomEdge;
                    elementToHeightSet.forEach(function(element) {
                      if (window.innerWidth < 768) {
                        finalHeightToReduce += mobileToolbarHeight;
                      }
                      element.style.maxHeight = 'calc(100vh - ' + finalHeightToReduce + 'px)';
                    })
                  }
                });
              }

              (function() {
                var desktopIndicator = document.querySelector('#navbar .navbar-account-dropdown .dropdown-toggle');
                var mobileIndicator = document.querySelector('.navbar .navbar-toggle');
                if (window.innerWidth > 767) {
                  if (desktopIndicator) {
                    desktopIndicator.addEventListener('mouseover', computeNotificationsMaxHeight);
                  }
                } else {
                  if (mobileIndicator) {
                    mobileIndicator.addEventListener('click', computeNotificationsMaxHeight);
                  }
                }
              })();
              //limited products toggle
              (function() {
                var limitedProductsIndicator = document.querySelector('#navbar .toggleLimitedProducts');
                if (limitedProductsIndicator) {
                  limitedProductsIndicator.addEventListener('click', function() {
                    this.classList.toggle('open');
                    computeNotificationsMaxHeight();
                  })
                }
              })();
            </script>

<script>
      $(function() {
        $('.obt-menu').click(function() {
          $('.menu-back.lvl2').removeClass('deactive');
          $('.obt-menu').removeClass('rolldown');
          $(this).addClass('rolldown');
          document.querySelector('.menu-back.lvl2 .back-info').textContent = this.querySelector('a').textContent;
          this.querySelector('.dropdown-menu').style.animation = 'menuLevelSecondOpen .5s';
        });

        $('.rm-toggle button').click(function() {
          document.querySelector('#responsiveMenu').style.animation = 'menuLevelFirstOpen .5s';
          document.querySelector('.menu-back.lvl1').style.animation = 'menuLevelSecondOpen .5s';
          Array.from(document.querySelectorAll('#responsiveMenu .nav-shop-new a')).forEach(function(content) {
            content.style.animation = 'menuOpacityOpen .5s ease 0s 1 normal both running';
          });
          $('#responsiveMenu').addClass('responsive-menu');
          $('.navbar.navbar-fixed-top').addClass('z10000');
          $('html').addClass('noscroll');
          $('.responsive-menu .lvl2').addClass('deactive');
          $('.responsive-menu .lvl3').addClass('deactive');
          $('.sugester-chat-tab').addClass('hidden');
        });

        $('.menu-back.lvl1').click(function() {
          document.querySelector('#responsiveMenu').style.animation = 'menuLevelFirstClose .5s ease 0s 1 normal both running';
          document.querySelector('.menu-back.lvl1').style.animation = 'menuLevelSecondClose .5s ease 0s 1 normal both running';
          Array.from(document.querySelectorAll('#responsiveMenu .nav-shop-new > li > a')).forEach(function(content) {
            content.style.animation = 'menuOpacityClose .5s ease 0s 1 normal both running';
          });
          $('.navbar.navbar-fixed-top').removeClass('z10000');
          $('html').removeClass('noscroll');
          $('.sugester-chat-tab').removeClass('hidden');
        });

        $('.menu-back.lvl2 i.back-i').click(function() {
          document.querySelector('.obt-menu.rolldown .dropdown-menu').style.animation = 'menuLevelSecondClose .5s ease 0s 1 normal both running';
          $('.menu-back.lvl2').addClass('deactive');
        });

        $('.menu-back.lvl2 i.close-i').click(function() {
          document.querySelector('.obt-menu.rolldown .dropdown-menu').style.animation = 'menuLevelSecondClose .5s ease 0s 1 normal both running';
          $('.menu-back.lvl2').addClass('deactive');

          document.querySelector('#responsiveMenu').style.animation = 'menuLevelFirstClose .5s ease 0s 1 normal both running';
          document.querySelector('.menu-back.lvl1').style.animation = 'menuLevelSecondClose .5s ease 0s 1 normal both running';
          Array.from(document.querySelectorAll('#responsiveMenu .nav-shop-new > li > a')).forEach(function(content) {
            content.style.animation = 'menuOpacityClose .5s ease 0s 1 normal both running';
          });
          $('.navbar.navbar-fixed-top').removeClass('z10000');
          $('html').removeClass('noscroll');
          $('.sugester-chat-tab').removeClass('hidden');
        });


          //lvl3 desktop
          if (window.innerWidth > 991) {
            var levelContainer = document.getElementById('menuLevel3');
            var lvl1Dropdowns = document.querySelectorAll('.obt-menu .dropdown-toggle');
            var lvl3IsOpen = false;
            //lvl2 to lvl3 link
            document.querySelectorAll('.toLevel3').forEach(function (button) {
              button.addEventListener('mouseover', function () {
                //remove previous lvl3 and bg
                if (document.querySelector('.hideLevel3')) {
                  document.querySelector('.hideLevel3').remove();
                }
                //position
                var position = 0;
                //calculate position from lvl1
                var margin = document.querySelector('#responsiveMenu').clientHeight;
                //calculate position from lvl2
                document.querySelectorAll('.dropdown-menu').forEach(function(menu){
                  var height = menu.scrollHeight;
                  if (height > position) {
                    position = height;
                  }
                });
                levelContainer.style.top = (position + margin) + 'px';
                //html paste
                var html = this.parentElement.querySelector('.menu-lvl-3').innerHTML;
                levelContainer.innerHTML = html + '';
                levelContainer.style.display = 'block';
                //hide lvl3
                var bg = document.createElement('div');
                bg.classList.add('hideLevel3');
                bg.addEventListener('mouseover', function(){
                  if (document.querySelector('.marked')) document.querySelector('.marked').classList.remove('marked');
                  lvl3IsOpen = false;
                  levelContainer.style.display = 'none';
                  this.remove();
                });
                document.getElementById('responsiveMenu').appendChild(bg);
                lvl3IsOpen = true;
              });
            });
            //lvl1 to lvl2 link
            lvl1Dropdowns.forEach(function (link) {
              link.addEventListener('mouseover', function () {
                //add class to dropdown
                if (document.querySelector('.marked')) document.querySelector('.marked').classList.remove('marked');
                this.parentElement.classList.add('marked');
                //close lvl3
                if (lvl3IsOpen) {
                  lvl3IsOpen = false;
                  levelContainer.style.display = 'none';
                  document.querySelector('.hideLevel3').remove();
                }
                //add class to lvl2 menu when mouseover lvl1 dropdown element and delete this class from previous element with it
                var previous = document.querySelector('.openNow');
                if (previous) previous.classList.remove('openNow');
                link.parentElement.querySelector('.dropdown-menu').classList.add('openNow');
              })
            });

            //force open lvl2 when mouseover lvl3
            levelContainer.addEventListener('mouseover', function () {
              if (lvl3IsOpen && window.innerWidth > 991) {
                document.querySelector('.dropdown-menu.openNow').classList.add('force-open');
              }
            });
            levelContainer.addEventListener('mouseout', function () {
              if (lvl3IsOpen && window.innerWidth > 991) {
                document.querySelector('.dropdown-menu.openNow').classList.remove('force-open');
              }
            });
            //hide lvl3 when mouse leave document
            document.addEventListener('mouseleave', function () {
              if (document.querySelector('.marked')) document.querySelector('.marked').classList.remove('marked');
              if (lvl3IsOpen) {
                lvl3IsOpen = false;
                levelContainer.style.display = 'none';
                document.querySelector('.hideLevel3').remove();
              }
            });
            //hide lvl3 when mouseover lvl1 link
            document.querySelectorAll('.main-nav>li').forEach(function (li) {
              li.addEventListener('mouseover', function () {
                if (!li.classList.contains('dropdown')) {
                  if (document.querySelector('.marked')) document.querySelector('.marked').classList.remove('marked');
                  if (lvl3IsOpen) {
                    lvl3IsOpen = false;
                    levelContainer.style.display = 'none';
                    document.querySelector('.hideLevel3').remove();
                  }
                }
              })
            })


          } else {

            //lvl3 mobile
            var level3Links = document.querySelectorAll('.shop-categories-drop-col>ul>li>a.toLevel3');
            var level2ListElements = document.querySelectorAll('.shop-categories-drop-col>ul>li');
            var level3Container = document.getElementById('menuLevel3');
            var menuBack3Level = document.querySelector('.menu-back.lvl3');
            //handle menu-back
            //back
            menuBack3Level.querySelector('.back-i').addEventListener('click',function () {
              menuBack3Level.classList.add('deactive');
              level3Container.classList.remove('open');
            });
            //close all
            menuBack3Level.querySelector('.close-i').addEventListener('click',function () {
              menuBack3Level.classList.add('deactive');
              level3Container.classList.remove('open');
              document.querySelector('.obt-menu.rolldown .dropdown-menu').style.animation = 'menuLevelSecondClose .5s ease 0s 1 normal both running';
              $('.menu-back.lvl2').addClass('deactive');
              document.querySelector('#responsiveMenu').style.animation = 'menuLevelFirstClose .5s ease 0s 1 normal both running';
              document.querySelector('.menu-back.lvl1').style.animation = 'menuLevelSecondClose .5s ease 0s 1 normal both running';
              document.querySelectorAll('#responsiveMenu .nav-shop-new > li > a').forEach(function(content) {
                content.style.animation = 'menuOpacityClose .5s ease 0s 1 normal both running';
              });
              $('.navbar.navbar-fixed-top').removeClass('z10000');
              $('html').removeClass('noscroll');
              $('.sugester-chat-tab').removeClass('hidden');
            });
            //prevent link open if there is lvl3
            level3Links.forEach(function (a) {
              a.addEventListener('click', function () {
                event.preventDefault();
              });
            });
            //html paste and show menu-back
            level2ListElements.forEach(function (level2ListElement) {
              if (level2ListElement.querySelector('.toLevel3')) {
                level2ListElement.addEventListener('click', function () {
                  //html paste
                  var html = this.parentElement.querySelector('.menu-lvl-3').innerHTML;
                  level3Container.innerHTML = html + '';
                  level3Container.style.display = 'block';
                  //menu-back
                  var name = this.querySelector('a.toLevel3').textContent;
                  menuBack3Level.querySelector('.back-info').textContent = name;
                  //show level3
                  setTimeout(function () {
                    level3Container.classList.add('open');
                    menuBack3Level.classList.remove('deactive');
                  }, 50);
                })
              }
            });
          }
      });
      </script>

<h3>
<span>T-shirt Size Chart</span></h3>
<div class="sizing-info pull-right">
<img src="/images/size_tshirt.png"/></div>
<ul class="product-info">
<li>
<strong>Fabric:</strong> 100% Cotton Jersey (Heather Grey has 10% polyester)</li>
<li>
<strong>Weight:</strong> Men: 185 g/m2 (White: 180 g/m2); Women: 150 g/m2 (White: 180 g/m2)</li>
<li>
		Preshrunk Jersey knit</li>
<li>
		Deluxe 30's Soft style yarns</li>
</ul>
<div class="sizing-info">
<p>
<strong>How to Get the perfect size ?</strong></p>
	Grab your favourite shirt, lay it flat and measure it's width and length just like shown on the picture. If you're between two sizes you should probably go for the larger one as shirts have tendency to shrink 1-2 cm over time !<br/>
	This sizing chart is approximate. Sizes may vary +/- 3cm</div>

<div class="tab-pane" id="size-3" role="tabpanel">
<h3><span>Hoodie back Size Chart</span></h3>
<ul class="product-info">
<li>
<strong>Material:</strong> 80% Combed Ring Spun Cotton + 20% Polyester</li>
<li>
<strong>Weight:</strong> 280 g/m2</li>
<li>
		Slimmer shoulders and leaner arms with front facing shoulder seams</li>
<li>
		Coverseamed armholes, cufs and hem</li>
<li>
		MP3 earphone access</li>
</ul>
<div class="sizing-info">
<h5>
		How to Get the perfect size ?</h5>
	To choose the correct size for you, measure your body as follows:<br/>
<br/>
<strong>CHEST</strong><br/>
	Measure around the fullest part, place the tape close under the arms and make sure the tape is flat across the back.<br/>
	If you're between two sizes you should probably go for the larger one as our hoodies and sweatshirts follow a slim fit. This sizing chart is approximate. Sizes may vary +/- 3cm</div>

		dostęp do słuchawek</li>
</ul>
<div class="sizing-info">
<h5>
		Jak dobrać odpowiedni rozmiar?</h5>
	Aby wybrać odpowiedni rozmiar, zmierz swoją klatkę piersiową jak pokazano na obrazku.<br/>
	Przyłoż miarkę do klatki piersiowej bezpośrednio pod pachami i zmierz jej obwód.<br/>
	Upewnij się, że miarka przylega płasko do ciała.<br/>
<br/>
	Miej na uwadze, że rozmiar bluzy może się różnić do 3cm zarówno pod względem długości jak i szerokości.<br/>

<strong>How to Get the perfect size ?</strong></p>
	Grab your favourite shirt, lay it flat and measure it's width and length just like shown on the picture. If you're between two sizes you should probably go for the larger one as shirts have tendency to shrink 1-2 cm over time !<br/>
	This sizing chart is approximate. Sizes may vary +/- 3cm</div>

<h5>Join teealert</h5>
<p>Did you ever miss any OtherTee you've wanted? Now, thanks to Tee ALERT, we'll inform you about every new tee on sale !</p>
<form action="https://app.freshmail.com/pl/actions/subscribe/" class="navbar-form" id="joinTeeAlert" method="post" role="search">
<input name="subscribers_list_hash" type="hidden" value="hrgpgpds7q"/>
<div class="input-group add-on input-block-level">
<input class="form-control t-alert freshmail_email" name="freshmail_email" onchange="notificationsAgreement('freshmail', 'joinTeeAlert')" placeholder="Enter e-mail address" type="text"/>
<button class="btn submit btn-default" onclick="notificationsAgreementCheck('freshmail', 'joinTeeAlert')" type="button">Subscribe</button>
</div>
<div class="privacy-policy">
<input class="termsOfServiceAndPrivacyPolicy" id="styled-checkbox" name="termsOfServiceAndPrivacyPolicy" onchange="notificationsAgreement('freshmail', 'joinTeeAlert')" type="checkbox" value="1"/>
            I accept the            <a href="/othertees/customer-service/terms-of-service/" target="_blank">Terms of Service</a> and have become acquainted with the            <a href="/othertees/customer-service/privacy-policy/" target="_blank">Privacy Policy</a>.
            <a class="newsletter-rules cursor-d">Read more</a>
</div>
</form>
<script type="text/javascript">
            function notificationsAgreement(platform, id) {
              var submit = $('#' + id + ' .submit');
              var validEmail = validateEmail($('#' + id + ' .' + platform + '_email').val());
              if ($('#' + id + ' .termsOfServiceAndPrivacyPolicy').is(":checked") && validEmail === true) {
                submit.attr('type', 'submit');
                if ('teeAlert' == id) {
                  submit.toggleClass('btn-gray btn-green');
                }
              }
              else {
                submit.attr('type', 'button');
                if ('teeAlert' == id && submit.hasClass('btn-green')) {
                  submit.toggleClass('btn-green btn-gray');
                }
              }
            }

            function notificationsAgreementCheck(platform, id) {
              var showTeeAlertModal = false;
              if (!$('#' + id + ' .termsOfServiceAndPrivacyPolicy').is(":checked")) {
                $('#TeeAlertTermsOfServiceAndPrivacyPolicy .modal-body').html(gettext_teealert_privacy_policy);
                showTeeAlertModal = true;
              }
              else {
                var validEmail = validateEmail($('#' + id + ' .' + platform + '_email').val());
                if (validEmail === false) {
                  $('#TeeAlertTermsOfServiceAndPrivacyPolicy .modal-body').html(gettext_teealert_email_validation);
                  showTeeAlertModal = true;
                }
              }

              if (showTeeAlertModal === true) {
                $('#TeeAlertTermsOfServiceAndPrivacyPolicy').modal({
                  show: 'show'
                });
              }
            }
          </script>
